# Skills · W · part 63

833 entries, work-prd to workbench. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **work-prd**: Work through an entire PRD by implementing its child issues one at a time in dependency order. Spawns a fresh agent for each issue, pauses for review between issues, and stops for HITL issues that need human input. Use when the user wants to implement a whole PRD, work through all issues in a PRD, or build out a full feature from its PRD. ([jonzenor/dotagents-spellbook](https://github.com/jonzenor/dotagents-spellbook/tree/HEAD/skills/work-prd))
- **work-prep**: Orchestrates the full preparation workflow for a ticket. Moves the ticket to in progress (assigning it to the user if unassigned) as soon as it is read, then calls /flagrare:intake (which gathers context from Jira/Notion/Figma, grounds the brief in the codebase via /flagrare:codebase-explore, then asks codebase-informed clarifying questions), then hands the codebase-grounded brief to /flagrare:atdd-plan for a TDD-first implementation plan. Use when the user shares a ticket key (e.g. PROJ-123), says 'start work on', 'pick up ticket', 'plan this feature', 'work prep', or provides a Jira URL. ([Flagrare/agent-skills](https://github.com/Flagrare/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/flagrare/skills/work-prep) · ★14)
- **work-prep**: Підготуватися до навчальної роботи (ПЗ, ЛБ, курсової) – зібрати специфікацію завдання з LMS і методички, скласти план вивчення теорії (для /learn), підготувати теку роботи і порожній каркас звіту з правильним оформленням. Сам зміст роботи і звіту пише студент. Використовувати на «підготуй ТВО ЛБ3», «з чого почати ПЗ2», «/work-prep». ([DmytroBabarytskyi/semester-kit](https://github.com/DmytroBabarytskyi/semester-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/work-prep))

- **work-preview-on-phone**: Put this project's running dev server on Simon's phone over Tailscale, with login working, in any repo.
